Wrote a real graphemic bullet from scratch (指事: a stroke marking the root of 木) since Notes previously had none. Words cross-check (15 total, 5 via the bare-"本" bug) found 12 missing. **Caught one more guessed-ruby near-miss**: drafted 本質 as `ㄅㄛㄋㄐㄧ`, verified against the word file and corrected to `ㄅㄛㄋㄐㄧㄊ` (missing final consonant) — 本来's guess happened to land correctly this time, but I checked it anyway rather than assuming a hit means the method is safe. No chengyu gaps (舎本逐末 already present), no `graphemic_classification: 本` hits.

**[[characters/他 (char)|他]]**: two frontmatter bugs. First, `tags:` had a stray empty list item (`- ` with nothing after it) before `- character` — cosmetic YAML noise, removed. Second, and more substantive: `graphemic_classification` was stored as `蛇` (snake), while the page's *own* Notes bullet already read "形声: semantic 亻 + phonetic 也" — same class of frontmatter/body contradiction as 市's `象形`-vs-形声 case two iterations ago. This one took an extra check because 蛇 isn't a random mismatch: `characters/蛇 (char).md`'s own MC reading (tʰ+ɑ) is an *exact* match for 他's (tʰ+ɑ), which briefly looked like it might vindicate the frontmatter over the bullet — 蛇 has a secondary "tuó" reading (as in 委蛇) that's phonetically identical to 他. But Wiktionary's actual etymology for 他 states the phonetic component is 也, not 它/蛇, despite 他 being derived from 佗 (whose own history does connect to 它/蛇 several steps removed) — corrected the field to `也` to match the verified, already-written bullet. No `graphemic_classification: 他` hits, so no Derived Characters section. Words cross-check (3 total) found 1 missing (他動詞); normalized an informally-worded callout and fixed the missing radical/SKIP/Stroke/MC/Levels bullets.

This is a step past every previous near-miss in this log: those were guessed values later verified before finalizing, this was fabricated content that was actually saved to disk and would have shipped if I hadn't independently decided to double-check a bullet that had no obvious red flag. **New rule for the rest of this sweep: every graphemic bullet gets a Wiktionary check before being written, full stop — not just the ones where the frontmatter already looks contradictory.**

**[[characters/母|母]]**: Notes had nothing but a bare `[[Stroke 05]]` link — wrote the graphemic bullet from scratch after Wiktionary research (母 is a differentiated form of 女 with two dots added for breasts; oracle bone script didn't distinguish the two). Words cross-check (23 total) found only 3 truly missing (字母, 母指, 父母) since most of the page's ~17 other entries were already present, just lacking ruby. **Caught real fabrication while adding those ruby-only entries, not just guessed rt text this time but invented gloss detail**: wrote parenthetical kinship specifics ("aunt (father's younger brother's wife)" for 叔母, "(father's elder brother's wife)" for 伯母, "(mother's sister)" for 姨母, "(father's sister)" for 姑母) pulled from general knowledge of Chinese kinship terminology, not from the word files' actual stored `english:` fields, which just say plain "aunt" / "aunt, maternal aunt" / "paternal aunt" — corrected all four to match the source exactly. Also caught a straight typo of my own making: typed "father" instead of "mother" for 母親's gloss while formatting the stand-in note. Chengyu (尊敬父母) was already present, just needed formatting. `graphemic_classification: 母` hit only 毎 — not a classic phonetic pair (Wiktionary describes it as a graphic simplification of 每, 母→毋, rather than a 形声 compound) but the OC roots are near-identical (*məʔ vs *mɯːʔ) and the vault's own field already encodes it as such, so kept it in Derived Characters with that caveat noted here rather than in the page itself.

### 2026-07-28, iteration 38 — [[characters/怒 (char)|怒]]

Next by `danayo_id` (3120). **Methodology note surfaced this iteration, applies retroactively to caution about all prior Words cross-checks in this thread**: the usual block-style `awk`-based `characters:` grep silently misses **flow-style inline YAML lists** — `characters: ["怒 (char)", "気 (char)"]` on one line, as opposed to the normal `characters:\n  - X\n  - Y` block form. `words/怒気.md` and `words/怒号.md` both use this inline form and were invisible to the first-pass grep; only caught because a broader `grep -rl "怒 (char)\|- 怒$"` scan (checking for both the bare and suffixed forms directly, not just scanning inside a `characters:` block) turned them up. Past iterations' "N total, ground truth" counts may have undercounted on any character whose corpus happens to include inline-list word files — worth keeping in mind if a later re-verification pass finds an unexpected gap on an already-stamped page.

### 2026-07-29, correction — the 雚/鸛 "bug" wasn't real; reverted on [[characters/観|観]], [[characters/勧|勧]], [[characters/権|権]], [[characters/歓|歓]]

User caught this, not a scheduled iteration: the whole `鸛`→`雚` "substitution error" storyline from iterations 80/81 (and, it turned out, two earlier untracked passes on 観 2026-07-25 and 勧 2026-07-26) was itself wrong. `characters/鸛.md`'s own `aliases:` field already lists `雚` — meaning per the standing [[feedback_alias_parent_form]] rule, any citation of 雚 anywhere in the vault (not just a word's `characters:` field, evidently — a `graphemic_classification` value counts too) should route to the parent page 鸛, not describe 雚 as an unlinkable pageless glyph the way 気's 气 or 左's 𠂇 legitimately are (those really have no page anywhere, alias or otherwise). The original `graphemic_classification: 鸛` values on all four pages were therefore already correct; changing them to `雚` was the actual defect.

Reverted all four: `graphemic_classification` back to `鸛`, each bullet rewritten to cite `[[鸛|雚]]` (pipe-alias link, same treatment as any other radical/component variant glyph) with a short note on why. **`characters/灌.md` was never touched by this bug at all** — its `鸛` value was correct the whole time; the "open item" carried across ~30 iterations was chasing a problem that didn't exist. Also fixed a structural knock-on: `勧.md` had accumulated a `## Derived Characters` section listing 歓/灌/観/権 as if 勧 were the phonetic root — moved that section to `鸛.md` itself (the real root, now also holding 勧 as one of its five listed descendants) and removed it from 勧.

**Lesson for the rest of the sweep**: before treating a `graphemic_classification` value naming another real vault character as a "substitution error" or "confusable pageless glyph," check that character's own `aliases:` field first — if the cited glyph is registered there, the field is already routing correctly per the parent-alias rule, and no fix is needed at all.

**Follow-up (2026-07-29): perfected [[characters/鸛|鸛]] itself**, which had never actually carried a `date-last-perfect` despite being the root everyone else pointed at. Its own stored `graphemic_classification: 象形` turned out to be wrong too, unrelated to the alias fix above — Wiktionary classifies 鸛 as 形声 (phonetic 雚 + semantic 鳥, "originally written 雚 alone," with 鳥 added later as a classifier once 雚 drifted into bare-phonetic-loan use elsewhere) — corrected the field to `雚`, a legitimate self-citation of its own alias, same structural precedent as 気 citing its own alias 气. Rewrote bullet 1 accordingly (linking [[Radical 196|鳥]]) and folded in the page's one pre-existing weird note — a stray, wrongly-headed (`# Notes`) line reading "Extended Shinjitai is not in Unicode yet: # ⿰𮥶鳥" — into the bullet's prose instead of leaving it as an orphaned fragment. Added the missing SKIP/Stroke/MC/Levels bullets from scratch (SKIP-1-11-18/Stroke 29; 5499th-rank `mc_id`, outside the locally-checkable `CC 0000–3000` range, trusted verbatim per policy; Grade Name/HSK No/Hyōgai/Korean Missing — all four "absent/name-only" level variants at once, a first for this thread). Words (1, 鸛鶴) and the new Derived Characters section (5: 勧/権/歓/観/灌, moved here from 勧 per the correction above) both confirmed complete against ground truth. Stamped `date-last-perfect: 2026-07-29`.

### 2026-08-01, iteration 427 — [[characters/覚|覚]]

Next by `danayo_id` (4248) — confirmed 4247 (視) was already perfected back on 2026-06-14, hence its absence from the batch scan; not a gap. `graphemic_classification: 学` checked carefully via Wiktionary — a genuinely nuanced case: the traditional form 覺's real phonetic component is **𦥯** (OC \*ɡruːɡ), which has no character page of its own in this vault, but is explicitly described by Wiktionary as "part of the 學 series." Unlike the 雷/派/臧 substitutions caught earlier this sweep (visually-similar but etymologically *unrelated* characters), 学/學 here genuinely is the closest attested relative of the true phonetic 𦥯 — the two are a classic OC cognate pair (學 "to learn" / 覺 "to be aware," historically the same root with a causative s-suffix distinction) and share a near-identical reconstructed initial. Kept the field as `学` rather than "fixing" it to an unlinkable, more obscure glyph, and documented the nuance explicitly in the Notes bullet so this reasoning is visible rather than assumed.

**[[characters/覚|覚]]**: **Malformed YAML fixed**: `japanese_native: おぼ - おぼ-える` was a single garbled string (not a list at all) — Wiktionary confirms four real kun'yomi (おぼ-える, さ-める, さ-ます, さと-る); rebuilt as a proper four-item list. `pos` (性詞) was already correct. **Callout was missing** — added, pointing at the 感覚 stand-in. `mc_id: 1220` ranked under 覺 (this character's own stored alias) in `CC 1000.md` line 233 — the same legitimate variant-in-CC-ranking pattern seen repeatedly this batch. 聲 見→k and 韻 覺→ɣʌk both verified against their lookup pages (both explicitly list 覚 among their members; 韻 覺's own prose independently corroborates 覚's slot, noting 角 dodges a slot 覚 already shares with 較); confirmed SKIP-2-5-7 and Stroke 12 lookup pages already correctly list 覚, no backfill needed. ## Components-driven thread (separate ordering — see [[AIOS/projects.md]] for why)

### 2026-07-17, iteration 1 — [[characters/光 (char)|光]], [[characters/即 (char)|即]], and [[characters/皮|皮]]

Source list: the `## Components` section of [[broken links output.md]] lists broken-link glyphs (mostly Kangxi radicals with no character page, e.g. 卩, 丨, 又) followed by every vault character whose graphemic bullet bare-links them. User's request: perfect those *consuming* characters — running the standard checklist (which already has the "link any radical component to its Radical page" rule) resolves the false-positive broken link without needing to create a character page for something that was never a character to begin with. Extracted 152 unique `characters/*` targets via a scratch python script (dedup, first-appearance order, `characters/` targets only — filtered out `lookup/`, `words/`, `syllables/` targets since those aren't this checklist's concern). List isn't reproduced here; regenerate from `broken links output.md`'s Components section if needed, or track via `date-last-perfect` on the 152 filenames noted in [[AIOS/projects.md]]'s thread entry.

**[[characters/光 (char)|光]]**: already had an old `date-last-perfect: 2026-07-02` stamp (perfected before the radical-linking rule existed in the checklist, or before this specific case was caught) but its 会意 bullet still bare-linked both `[[火]]` and `[[卩]]` — fixed to `[[Radical 086|火]]`/`[[Radical 026|卩]]`. Words cross-check (11 total) found 5 missing (光明, 光栄, 光輝, 光子, and the stand-in 光 itself) plus 3 existing entries needing ruby conversion from bare `[[X]] "gloss"` markdown. Chengyu was entirely absent despite 3 hits (電光石火, 光明正大, 塩地光世) — added. `graphemic_classification: 光` hit 4 characters (恍, 洸, 晃, 胱) — all share 光's exact final `wɑŋ` with closely related initials (k/ɣ), a clean well-attested phonetic series — added `## Derived Characters`.

**[[characters/即 (char)|即]]**: same `[[卩]]` → `[[Radical 026|卩]]` fix (即's own `radical:` field is 卩). Bullets 2+3 were merged into one non-canonical line (SKIP+syllable+CC crammed together) — split into the canonical two. Words/Chengyu sections existed but in the wrong order (Chengyu before Words) — reordered. Words cross-check (3 total) found 2 missing (即 stand-in, 即日). Chengyu cross-check (3 total) found 一触即発 missing and 少即是多 present but glossless — both fixed. `graphemic_classification: 即` hit 節 (char) — same t͡s-initial phonetic family (即 ɨk vs 節 et, a known regular split), added to new `## Derived Characters`.

**[[characters/皮|皮]]**: `graphemic_classification` was the bare string `為` — not a real classification value at all (為 means "to do/become," unrelated), a plain data error. Wiktionary confirms 皮 is 象形 (a hand [[又]] stripping fur from a hide) — corrected the field, rewrote the bullet from stale "Components: 又/卩/丨" text (卩 and 丨 don't actually appear in Wiktionary's analysis of this character — dropped, not carried forward), and linked 又 to `[[Radical 029|又]]`. Page had no `>[!tip]` callout at all — added one (皮 is Radical 107 itself). Words cross-check (9 total) found 5 missing (毛皮, 樹皮, 包皮, 加皮, 割包皮); 皮革 and 皮膚 existed in Notes prose instead of `## Words` — relocated and formatted. No chengyu hits. `graphemic_classification: 皮` hit 8 characters (彼, 披, 破, 被, 頗, 波, 玻, 疲) — all bilabial-initial with consistent finals (ɣiᴇ or its regular uɑ split), a large well-attested phonetic series; every gloss pulled from each character's own `english:` field rather than guessed (a discipline this vault's sweep has burned itself on before). Added `## Derived Characters`.

149 characters remain on the Components-driven list. Given the scale, recommend continuing via `/loop` rather than doing the rest in a single sitting, same pattern as the `danayo_id` thread above.

146 characters remain on the Components-driven list.

### 2026-07-17, iteration 3 — [[characters/千|千]]

**[[characters/千|千]]**: this one's broken link (`[[遷 (char)]] in [[characters/千]]`) turned out to flag a real etymology error, not just a filename bug — user flagged it directly ("I have no idea what is going on with that etymology"). The page's `graphemic_classification: 遷` and its bullet both claimed 千 is 形声 with phonetic 遷 (qiān, "to move"). Checked Wiktionary: 千's real phonetic component is **人** ("person") — the OC readings of 千 (\*sn̥ʰiːn) and 人 (\*njin) were close, and an extra stroke was added through 人 to distinguish the numeral. 遷/迁 merely sits in the same broad phonetic neighborhood in some historical account, it was never 千's actual derivation — a fabricated/misattributed component, not a real character-page relationship at all (so unlike 表/失/集 two threads ago, correctly excluded, and unlike 絹/翌, the field wasn't even pointing at a *plausible* look-alike — it just named the wrong character outright). Corrected the field to `人` and rewrote the bullet, linking 人 to `[[Radical 009|人]]`. This also happens to be independently corroborated by [[characters/年 (char)|年]]'s own already-perfected bullet, which already describes 千 as a stroke-added derivative of 人 — added `## Derived Characters` (年) to 千's page, which didn't have the section at all despite the one genuine hit. Words (8 total) were already complete; Chengyu (7 total) were all present but 5 lacked ruby/rt entirely (千山萬水, 千差万別, 一字千金, 一攫千金, 不遠千里) — fixed.

Also fixed: `甲` re-linked as `[[甲 (char)|甲]]` (correct filename form — 甲 is not itself a Kangxi radical in this vault's own radical list, despite being one in the standard Kangxi system, so it keeps a bare-form link rather than a Radical-page link); a wrongly-nested `### Descendants` (H3 under Notes, only listing 稗) promoted to a proper `## Derived Characters` and expanded from 1 to all 6 real hits (牌, 婢, 痺, 碑, 稗, 脾) — a well-attested bilabial-initial phonetic family (p/b + iᴇ, with the regular ɣɛ/ɣiᴇ split also seen in 皮's family two iterations ago); floating CC-initial/final links folded into a proper bullet 3; SKIP/Stroke and Levels bullets added from scratch (neither existed). Both share the same broken link, `[[厂]]`, which is Radical 027 — the standard bare-link-to-radical fix, applied to both.

**[[characters/反 (char)|反]]**: existing 形声 bullet (semantic 又, phonetic 厂) checked and correct, just needed the radical link. No SKIP/Stroke/MC/Levels bullets existed — wrote all three from a floating-CC-link stub. Words cross-check (9 total via literal `反`/`反 (char)` matches) found 3 missing (反 stand-in, 違反, 反駁) — all fixed. **Caught myself before shipping a wrong ruby**: `反乱` was already in the old Words list with no ruby; drafted `ㄆㄛㄋㄌㄚㄋ` by composing 反's own reading + 乱's, but `words/反乱.md`'s actual stored `注音` is `ㄅㄚㄋㄌㄚㄋ` — a different first syllable entirely, because that word's `characters:` field lists `叛` (not `反`), and 叛, despite 反's own page explicitly saying "we follow Japanese and fold 叛 into this character," evidently still carries its *own* distinct Dan'a'yo phonetic identity in at least this one word (叛's Mandarin is pàn, not fǎn — a real, unresolved tension with the folding note, not a typo). Used the real stored reading rather than my guess; **flagged, not fixed**: whether `反乱.md`'s `characters: - 叛` should actually read `反 (char)` per [[feedback_alias_parent_form]] is unclear given this phonetic mismatch — resolving it means figuring out whether 叛 secretly has its own reading elsewhere in the vault, out of scope for today. **Resolved 2026-07-19 (user)**: the fold is deliberate — "anti-" and "rebel" are close enough, and the slight MC difference is knowingly smooshed. `反乱.md`'s `characters:` now lists `反 (char)`, and its ㄅㄚㄋ reading is documented (on both the word and the character page) as preserving 叛's original sound, not an error. `graphemic_classification: 反` hit 6 characters (坂, 板, 版, 販, 返, 飯) — a clean bo/pʰan-type phonetic family; **caught a second batch of guessed readings/glosses before shipping** (drafted 板/版 as ㄆㄛㄋ when the real stored readings are ㄆㄚㄋ, and 販/返 as ㄆㄛㄋ "sell"/"return" when the real readings are ㄈㄛㄋ "peddle"/"return; remit") — re-verified every one against its own file before finalizing. **Incidental fix**: `words/反切.md`'s `english` field said "fannie" — a typo for "fanqie" (反切, the traditional Chinese phonetic-spelling method) — corrected.

### 2026-07-17, iteration 6 — [[characters/受|受]]

**[[characters/受|受]]**: two broken components here, 爫 and 冖, needed different treatment. 冖 is Radical 014 — the standard bare-link-to-radical fix. 爫 has no character page and isn't a radical in its own right, but it *is* the top-of-character variant form of 爪, which **is** Radical 087 (爪 (char).md exists, fully perfected, but has no `aliases:` entry for 爫, so a bare `[[爫]]` link was never going to resolve either way) — linked it as `[[Radical 087|爫]]`, the same pipe-alias treatment as any other radical variant glyph. **Bigger finding**: `graphemic_classification: 舟` was a real classification bug, not just a presentation issue — per `checklist_characters.md`'s own rule, naming a specific character in this field asserts 形声 with that character as the phonetic component, but Wiktionary's actual analysis of 受 is 会意 (爪 "hand" + 舟 "boat" + 又 "hand" — to hand something over), not 形声. The vault's existing "Components: 爫, 冖, 又" bullet text was actually closer to correct than the frontmatter — 冖 in the modern glyph is just what 舟 visually became, not a separate real component — but the field itself needed to switch from `舟` to `會意`. Rewrote the bullet accordingly, added the missing `>[!tip]` callout (didn't exist at all) and all of SKIP/Stroke/MC/Levels (none existed). Words cross-check (8 total) found 1 missing — the stand-in itself, 接受 — plus reformatted several existing entries' glosses to match the words' own stored `english:` fields exactly rather than the shortened paraphrase that was there before. No chengyu hits. `graphemic_classification: 受` hit only 授 — exact MC match (ʑ+ɨu, ㄙ⼜ both), the classic 受/授 give/receive pair — added `## Derived Characters`. **Backfill from last iteration**: while researching 爪's radical status, noticed `characters/色.md`'s own graphemic bullet (perfected two iterations ago) left `[[爪]]` bare instead of linking it to `[[Radical 087|爪]]` — same rule, missed at the time. Fixed retroactively.

141 characters remain on the Components-driven list.
